## PREMISES ALREADY SETTLED, RE-INLINED (each is a QUESTION if you can check it)

1. **The fix** (unchanged in substance since pass 1): the `default:` arm no longer calls `exit(1)`; a READ keeps the
   byte the memcpy served; a WRITE is DECLINED (member 3, not member 6 -- the cased siblings contradict a blanket
   store); one latched `fatal()` per offset per instance (`uint64_t warned_unimpl`, bit `relative_addr`, which is
   folded `%= PCC2_SIZE` (0x40) as the handler's first statement so the shift is 0..63 on a 64-bit type).  43 of the
   0x40 offsets reach the arm: 18 named-unimplemented, 12 counter interiors, 13 the fork header leaves unnamed (the
   MANUAL names some of those: 0x39 is SPEED's second byte, 0x3b PRTDATA's low byte).
2. **The repair to the product** is confined to the arm: both announces gain `(len %d)`; the read announce prints
   `data[0]` -- the byte actually SERVED -- instead of reloading `d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]`, because the SIGALRM
   tick rewrites the counter interiors between the memcpy and the announce (codex), and a wide read is labelled.
   `data` was filled by the memcpy above the switch (or zero-filled on the #119 end-span path, where `data[0]` is
   then truthfully 0x00).  Three comment sentences corrected ("no guest reaches" -> the inspected OpenBSD driver
   paths; "the array's default, not a computed value" -> the array's CURRENT byte, the tick's for an interior;
   "four arms up" -> above).
3. **The six escapes of the 23-row instrument** and the row that now kills each:
     Opus M_O1  read announce value -> 0 (MEASURED 23/23)              -> `srvd val text` (wants `served 0x34`)
     Opus M_O2  member 6 on the LATCHED path (MEASURED 23/23)          -> `sameoff val` (wants 0x0, reads 0x5a)
     grok A     latch SET only on writes                                -> `latch rd rd`, `latch rd wr` (want 1, print 2)
     grok B     `if (len != 1) exit(1);`                                -> `wide wr`, `wide rd` (host exit)
     codex      `|=` -> `=` at the latch update                          -> `latch aba` (wants 2, prints 3)
     Fable      `if (relative_addr == 0x06) exit(1);`                   -> `inword wr`/`inword rd` (all 12 interiors now)
   In table 3 each of those six is scored by BOTH instruments: the pass-2 one (23/23 = the breach, measured) and the
   repaired one (red).  BASE is measured full on both (the old anchors survive the `(len N)` text).
4. **The setter x tester product**: the body tests `writeflag` in the announce and the guard tests the latch bit;
   because the SET is unconditional, a mutant can condition it -- so the SETTER's direction is a real axis.  Rows:
   W,W `latch sameoff`; W,R `latch rw one`; R,R `latch rd rd`; R,W `latch rd wr`; accumulation `latch aba`.  `len` is

## QUESTIONS

1. **IS THE REPAIR RIGHT?**  The arm (inlined, every exit path): is `data[0]` always the byte the guest received --
   on the memcpy path AND the end-span zero-fill path; for a WRITE is `data` unused by the announce (it prints idata);
   does `(int) len` fit; does any row's text anchor break (`offset 0x2a`, `served 0x`, the direction phrases, MSG)?
2. **DOES EACH OF THE TWELVE MUTANTS DIE FOR THE REASON CLAIMED?**  Table 3 and its predictions are inlined; the six
   pass-2 mutants carry a second score on the pass-2 instrument (23/23 owed = the breach).  A prediction that missed
   is the interesting case; a mutant killed by a row OTHER than the one built for it is a row killing by accident.
3. **THE CLOSURE**: setter x tester (four cells), accumulation, latched-path disposition, served value, width both
   directions, every offset both directions.  Is any cell still open?  Name the mutant.  Is anything in the
   DISCLOSED list actually floor (a predicate the arm tests)?
4. **THE SMALLEST EDIT THAT BREAKS THE PROPERTY AND PASSES 36 ROWS.**  Name the row you expect to stay green and why.
   (Opus: the two pass-2 escapes were found by BUILDING; a derived escape is a prediction until measured.)
5. **IS THE RECORD HONEST?**  The CHANGELOG block (pass 2 + repair sections inlined), the contract (docstring), the
   pass-3 PRETABLE.  Name any sentence a measurement refutes, any count without a deriver.
6. **CLOSE?**  A closing zero must be VALID: the diff delivered (inlined), the roster complete or misses named, the
   zero carried WITH its denominator -- which mutant shapes (twelve, named), which cells.  If you say close, say
   what the denominator EXCLUDES.

If, while verifying a claim, you found something ELSE -- say so.

## THE ARM AS IT NOW STANDS (from `default:` to the handler's `return 1`, VERBATIM)

```c
	default:
		/*  #501 (pcc2defaultkill): this arm ended the host with exit(1) on ANY
		    access to an offset with no case -- 43 of the 0x40 byte offsets:
		    eighteen documented registers the model does not implement (IEICR,
		    IEERR, IEBERR, GPIO, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, the *IACKs),
		    the bytes inside the four counter/compare words, and the unnamed
		    ones -- READS included, though the memcpy above the switch had
		    already served them.  A guest ld.bu or st.b at any of them was a host
		    kill (rung-3 witness).  Now: a READ keeps the served byte (the array's
		    current byte, said so -- for a counter interior that is the tick's
		    value, not a default); a WRITE is DECLINED.  Not a
		    backed store: the cased siblings contradict one (SCCERR clears on
		    write, the ICRs mask and reassert, PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are
		    read-only), no inspected OpenBSD driver path reaches them (if_ie never
		    attaches: unreadable sits at its 82596 address), and a store from the
		    one arm 43 offsets reach would add a bounded-write surface.  One
		    latched fatal() per offset per instance, the house `(once)' shape --
		    unlike PSCALEADJ's UNLATCHED print above (pcc2pscaleflood).  The
		    IACK offsets 0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest: modelling them (on
		    the SCCRXIACK shape) would open two new entries into the C040 path,
		    so pcc2c040kill goes first.  The %= fold above IS the real part's:
		    Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists $FFF42040-$FFF42FFF as repeated
		    PCCchip2 registers.  */
		if (!(d->warned_unimpl & (1ULL << relative_addr))) {
			d->warned_unimpl |= 1ULL << relative_addr;
			if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
				fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented write to offset "
				    "0x%x (len %d): 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\n",
				    (int) relative_addr, (int) len, (int) idata);
			else
				/*  data[0] is the byte actually SERVED; a reload of the
				    array here could differ (the tick rewrites the
				    counter interiors), and a wide read is labelled.  */
				fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented read from offset "
				    "0x%x (len %d) -- served 0x%02x.  (once) ]\n",
				    (int) relative_addr, (int) len, data[0]);
		}
		break;
	}

	return 1;
```

```diff
+reaches these offsets on this model's inspected driver paths -- if_ie never attaches), and a store into pcctwo_reg[] from the

+CONTRACT -- what this detector is built to catch, by row (36 rows; the 13 added by the pass-2 repair are marked +):
+  (b) the verbatim `exit(1)' reinstated             -> every survival row (named/inword/unnamed wr AND rd, alias, wide)
```

## THE DETECTOR (`regress/pcc2_default_probe.py`, VERBATIM, contract included)

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""pcc2defaultkill DETECTOR (#501) -- the `default:' arm of DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) in dev_pcc2.c.

Before #501 that arm ended the emulator process (`exit(1)') on ANY access to an offset with no `case'
-- READS included, although the memcpy above the switch had already served them.  Forty-three of the
0x40 byte offsets reach it (derived from mvme_pcctworeg.h against the case list): eighteen NAMED
registers the model does not implement (PSCALECNT, GPIO_ICR/PCR, SCCMOIACK, SCCTXIACK, IEERR, IEICR,
IEBERR, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, PRTDATA), the bytes INSIDE the four 32-bit counter/compare
words, and the unnamed offsets.  A single guest `st.b' or `ld.bu' at any of them was a host kill
(rung-3 witness, 7/13 on the committed build: regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/witness_20260905/).

THE FIX (pass-1 panel of 2026-09-05, nine seats, converged): READ side -- keep the byte the memcpy
already served (the register array's CURRENT byte -- the(sys.argv) > 2 else "local"
DUMMY = "/tmp/pcc2_dummy.bin"
if not os.path.exists(DUMMY):
    open(DUMMY, "wb").write(b"\0" * 4096)
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
_w = io.open(os.path.join(HERE, "pcc2_width_probe.py"), encoding="utf-8").read()
exec(_w[_w.index("def session("):_w.index("\ndef printed")])     # session(cmds) -> (transcript, alive, how)

CHIPID, GENCTL, T1CMP, SCCERR = 0x00, 0x02, 0x04, 0x1c
IEERR, IEICR, IEBERR, SCCTXIACK, PRTICR = 0x28, 0x2a, 0x2b, 0x25, 0x30
NAMED18 = [0x14, 0x18, 0x19, 0x23, 0x25, 0x28, 0x2a, 0x2b, 0x2c, 0x30, 0x31, 0x32, 0x33, 0x34, 0x36, 0x37, 0x38, 0x3a]
INWORD = [0x05, 0x06, 0x07, 0x09, 0x0a, 0x0b, 0x0d, 0x0e, 0x0f, 0x11, 0x12, 0x13]   # all 12 counter interiors
UNNAMED = [0x20, 0x21, 0x22, 0x24, 0x26, 0x29, 0x2d, 0x2e, 0x35, 0x39, 0x3b, 0x3c, 0x3d]   # all 13 the fork header leaves unnamed
assert len(NAMED18) + len(INWORD) + len(UNNAMED) == 43 and len(set(NAMED18 + INWORD + UNNAMED)) == 43
MSG = "pcc2: unimplemented"
def enc(op26, rd, rs1, imm): return (op26 << 26) | (rd << 21) | (rs1 << 16) | (imm & 0xffff)
def LD_BU(rd, off): return enc(0x03, rd, 2, off)
def LD_W(rd, off): return enc(0x05, rd, 2, off)
def ST_B(rs, off): return enc(0x0b, rs, 2, off)
def ST_W(rs, off): return enc(0x09, rs, 2, off)
I_ST_SCR = enc(0x09, 5, 3, 0); I_LD_SCR = enc(0x05, 4, 3, 0); I_BR_SELF = 0xc0000000
def program(insns, at=CODE):
    words = list(insns) + [I_BR_SELF, 0]
    return ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * k, w) for k, w in enumerate(words)] + ["pc=0x%x" % at, "step %d" % len(insns)]
def setup(r5="0x5a5a5a5a"):
    return ["r2=0x%x" % PCC2, "r3=0x%x" % SCRATCH, "r5=%s" % r5, "r4=0x%x" % SENTINEL]
def printed(t, reg="r4"):
    m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\r?\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
    return "0x%x" % int(m.group(1), 16) if m else "absent"
def fate(alive, how): return "survived" if alive else "host " + how
def val(t, alive, how): return printed(t) if alive else fate(alive, how)
rows, bad = [], []
def row(name, got, want, why):
    ok = str(got) == str(want)
    rows.append(name); (None if ok else bad.append(name))
    #  STATUS LAST: gate 14 counts a row with `^<name>  .*ok$' / ` ok$' / ` FAIL$' -- a first draft printed the
    #  status etup() + program([LD_BU(4, o) for o in NAMED18]))
row("named rd", fate(alive, how), "survived", "guest ld.bu at all 18 in one session: the READ side of the defect; a writeflag guard that re-kills reads dies here")
row("named rd msg", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 18, "the read announce fires once per offset too")
row("named rd srvd", t.count("served 0x") if alive else "n/a", 18, "the read announce says what happened -- SERVED, never 'ignored' (the VALUE is pinned by `srvd val text')")
t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, o) for o in INWORD]))

t, alive, how = ST_B(5, IEICR)]))
row("latch rd wr", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 1, "a READ then a write at one offset announce once: the read's bit silences the write (the fourth setter x tester cell)")
t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), ST_B(5, IEBERR), ST_B(5, IEICR)]))

row("srvd val", val(t, alive, how), "0x34", "the served byte is the array's CURRENT byte, here one the guest wrote through the cased word arm -- not a default")
row("srvd val text", t.count("served 0x34") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...and the announce prints THAT byte (data[0], what was served): `served 0x%02x' of 0 passes `named rd srvd' (Opus M_O1, MEASURED)")
# -- wide accesses at default offsets: the old kill was width-agnostic, so a `len != 1' re-kill is a partial reinstatement (grok B)
t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a5a5a5a") + program([ST_W(5, IEERR), LD_W(4, IEERR)]) + ["print r4"])
row("wide wr", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "a guest st.w at IEERR (0x28..0x2b, all uncased) survives and is declined: reads back 0x0 (a memcpy-backed store reads 0x5a5a5a5a)")
row("wide wr msg", t.count("offset 0x28 (len 4)") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...announced once, with the width; 5, GENCTL), LD_BU(4, GENCTL)]) + ["print r4"])

row("put b ctl msg", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 0, "...so it prints nothing -- not a witness, a control")
print("PCC2D_RESULT=%d/%d" % (len(rows) - len(bad), len(rows)))
if bad:
    print("PCC2D_RED=%s" % ",".join(bad))
```

## THE GATE SECTION (`git diff -- regress/gate_arm.sh`, VERBATIM; the whole #501 section is new against HEAD)

```diff
diff --git a/regress/gate_arm.sh b/regress/gate_arm.sh
index 65930c2..c542455 100644
--- a/regress/gate_arm.sh
+++ b/regress/gate_arm.sh

 fi
 
 
+#  ------------------------------------------------------------ pcc2defaultkill (#501)
+#  The default: arm of DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) ended the host on any access to an uncased
+#  offset, reads included.  Now: reads keep the served byte, writes are declined, one
+#  latched announce per offset per instance.  The witness (regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/
+#  witness_20260905/, 7/13 on the committed build) is NOT run here; this is its detector.
+PCC2DLOG=$LOGDIR/pcc2_default.log
+python3 -u pcc2_default_probe.py "$PMAX" gate > "$PCC2DLOG" 2>&1 || true
+if ! grep -q "PCC2D_PROBE_DONE" "$PCC2DLOG"; then
+    check "pcc2 default  by a per-offset latched `fatal()` (`uint64_t warned_unimpl`, one bit per byte offset) that
says DECLINED for a write and SERVED 0x%02x for a read, then `break` to the handler's single `return 1`.  Detector:
`regress/pcc2_default_probe.py`, 23 rows, run by gate 14.  Witness: rung 3, measured before the edit.**

## The defect and its witness

Forty-three of the 0x40 byte offsets have no `case` (derived from `mvme_pcctworeg.h` against the 21 labels; two seats
re-derived it): eighteen NAMED registers the model does not implement (PSCALECNT, GPIO_ICR/PCR, SCCMOIACK, SCCTXIACK,
IEERR, IEICR, IEBERR, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, PRTDATA), the twelve bytes INSIDE the four 32-bit
counter/compare words, and thirteen unnamed offsets.  Any access to one of them -- a READ included, although the
memcpy above the switch had already served it -- reached `default:` and `exit(1)`.

**The witness (`regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/witness_20260905/`), measured on the committed build BEFORE the
edit: 7/13.**  A guest `st.b` to IEICR (0x2a) ends the host with exit status 1, the arm's own `pcc2: unimplemented`
line printed first (the arm was reached); a guest `ld.bu` there likewise -- the READ side kills too; IEBERR and the
in-word byte 0x05 and the alias 0x6a kill the samered false; corrected).  The
witness script is the width probe's own `session()` helper: a first draft hand-rolled the pty wait, accepted a
STALE prompt, and read every kill as "survived" -- the carrier's rule about hand-rolled pty plumbing, re-learned.
The combined IEBERR/IEERR row measures IEBERR only (the exiting build dies at the first); IEERR's kill follows from
source (codex).  **13/13 on the fixed build.**

## The design (pass 1: nine seats, `_scratchpad/panel_20260905_021751`, converged)

READ: keep the byte the memcpy served and SAY it is served -- it is the register array's default, not a computed
value, so this is not the option set's member 4 and a bounded announce about it is not a wrong record.  WRITE:
member 3, DECLINE + latched announce -- NOT member 6 (backed accept), on three warrants the seats measured or read:
the file's own cased siblings contradict a blanket store (SCCERR is write-to-clear -- the board manual's SCLR bits
read as zero; the ICRs mask and reassert; PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are read-only); the addition licence is unmet
(no guest reaches these offsets on this model); a store from the one arm 43 offsets reach adds a bounded-write
surface into `pcctwo_reg[]` at 0x3d.  The Opus seat BUILT both candidates in a scratch clone and measured the
13-row witness at 13/13 under BOTH -- disposition-blind -- so the detector carries read-after-write rows in ONE
session (`ieicr rdawr`, `ieerr rdawr`: store 0x5a then load, want 0x0 -- a backed accept reads 0x5a) with the CASED
write-to-clear sibling `sccerr rdawr` as the control that reads 0x0 under either.

**Two premises of the pass-1 brief were wrong and are corrected here.**  The in-tree guest driver (`if_ie.c`) writes
three of these offsets, but NEVER RUNS on this model: `machine_mvme88k.c:106` installs `unreadable` at the 82596
address, `if_ie.c:407-409` fails its match on `badpaddr()` there, and `ie_setupram()` returns early at `:561-564`
before the PCC2 store at `:609` (Fable, Opus, grok, codex independently; verified).  `ie0` IS configured in
`conf/M187` and `conf/NFS` -- the brief's conf grep looked in the wrong files.  So the HIGH ranking rests on the
witness and severity, not on the driver.  And the alias the model implements (`relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE`) is the
REAL part's: Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists `$FFF42040-$FFF42FFF  PCCchip2 Registers  Repeated` -- the codex seat
cited it, the manual was fetched into `_scratchpad/` and the line read (`vmesbcp2.txt:4629`).  Two seats had rightly
called the fold unestablished before the manual was on disk.

Recorded alternatives: the codex seat's ONE latch for the whole site, writes only (not adopted: a driver scanning
registers should see each distinct one announced; both forms are bounded).  Corrections to the option set from this
round: `return 0` denotes a FAILED TRANSACTION, not inherently unmapped hardware (an IACK cycle with no interrupt
pending times out on the real part).  Disclosed: the in-word bytes are the interior of IMPLEMENTED counters, declined
here as a width/alignment matter (`pcc2widecompose`), not claimed as unimplemented registers; the IACK offsets
0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest -- modelling them on the SCCRXIACK shape would open two new entries into the
C040 path, so `pcc2c040kill` goes first (filed as a cross-round obligation); per-instance latch scope is not
provable by two fresh sessions (each is a new process) -- a two-instance fixture is filed.

## The detector (`regress/pcc2_default_probe.py`, 23 rows; gate 14 pins them)

Controls: `live`, `devsig chipid`, `sccerr rdawr` (cased sibling), `genctl rt`, `t1cmp word rt` (the cased arms are
untouched), `put b ctl` + `put b ctl msg` (the debugger path never reaches the arm).  Survival: `named wr`, `named rd`
(all eighteen named offsets in ONE session per direction -- valid because the fix has no per-offset branch;
`named wr msg` localises), `inword wr`, `unnamed wr`, `alias 0x6a`.  Disposition: `ieicr rdawr`, `ieerr rdawr` (0x0,
not the stored byte), `named rd srvd` (the read announce says SERVED, never ignored).  Latch: `named wr msg` 18,
`named rd msg` 18, `latch sameoff` 1, `latch twooff` 2, `latch rw one` 1.  Text: `alias msg` (the announce names the
FOLDED offset), `dir wr text`, `dir rd text`.  The pty helper is the width probe's, extracted at run time -- the
frozen instrument is therefore a PAIR (both files copied beside the table).

## The kill table (`regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/killtable_20260905/driver.log`; predictions in `PRETABLE_501.out`, mtime 04:12:20; run 1 launched 04:12:43, frozen default probe c76e053a; run 2 -- the table quoted -- frozen a96b8bcf, the status-last instrument gate 14 counts; width helper a080e2bc in both)

| run | predicted | measured | red rows |
|---|---|---|---|
| BASE (fresh clone of build/, rebuilt) | 23/23 | **23/23** | -- |
| `M_EXIT` -- the verbatim `exit(1)` body reinstated | 8/23 | **7/23** | named wr, named wr msg, named rd, named rd msg, named rd srvd, inword wr, unnamed wr, ieicr rdawr, ieerr rdawr, latch sameoff, latch twooff, latch rw one, alias 0x6a, alias msg, dir wr text, dir rd text -- sixteen (the prediction listed fifteen and miscounted its own green set) |
| `M_UNLATCH` -- `if (1)` for the latch test | 21/23 | **21/23** | latch sameoff 2, latch rw one 2 -- exact |
| `M_ONELATCH` -- one whole-arm bit (`1ULL`) | 19/23 | **19/23** | named wr msg 1, named rd msg 1, named rd srvd 1, latch twooff 1 -- exact |
| `M_BACKED` -- a store into `pcctwo_reg[]` added (member 6) | 21/23 | **21/23** | ieicr rdawr 0x5a, ieerr rdawr 0x5a -- exact; `sccerr rdawr` (the cased write-to-clear control) stayed 0x0 |
| `M_RDKILL` -- `if (writeflag == MEM_READ) exit(1);` | 18/23 | **16/23** | named rd, named rd msg, named rd srvd, dir rd text, latch rw one (the read in that session exits) -- as predicted -- plus ieicr rdawr and ieerr rdawr, whose store-then-LOAD sessions exit at the load |
| `M_DIRSWAP` -- the announce's direction test inverted | 20/23 | **20/23** | dir wr text 0, dir rd text 0, named rd srvd 0 -- exact |

Six of six killed; four predictions exact, two missed in the SAFE direction (more reds), each derived: M_EXIT's
prediction listed fifteen red rows against a green set of seven and so summed to 22 of 23 -- an arithmetic slip in the
pre-table, not a channel surprise; M_RDKILL's read-after-write rows contain a LOAD, so they die with the reads.  Every
mutant string was gcc-syntax-checked before the table (`mut_syntax2.py`, anchors from the driver's own `mut` lines) and
proven by a diff count against SEC; the clone restored from SEC, rebuilt, cmp-clean, sentinel cleared.  The
disposition-pinning rows did their one job: `M_BACKED` reads back the stored byte at both uncased offsets while the
cased sibling reads 0x0 -- the difference between member 6 and member 3, measured, with its control.

**Two runs, one prediction file.**  Run 1 (04:12-04:25, frozen default probe `c76e053a`) is the table above; its row
PRINT format put the status before the note, and gate 14 -- which counts rows by `^<name>  .*ok$` -- then counted
ZERO of 23 rows while the probe said DONE (`killtable_20260905/gate14_501_FAIL_rowformat.log`, 25 of 844 checks
red, the 251 width rows all green).  L1, fixed in-round: status last, as the width probe prints; the gate's own
patterns then count 23.  Run 2 (05:05-05:18, frozen `a96b8bcf`) re-measured the same six mutants on the fixed-format
instrument -- BASE 23, M_EXIT 7, M_UNLATCH 21, M_ONELATCH 19, M_BACKED 21, M_RDKILL 16, M_DIRSWAP 20 -- identical
to run 1 row for row; run 1 is kept as `run1_oldrowformat/`.  *A probe whose summary line is right and whose rows
the gate cannot count is a detector that would have shipped green and counted nothing; the gate is the check
that saw it, which is what a gate is for.*

## Gate 14 (`killtable_20260905/gate14_501.log`; the live probes -- default md5 a96b8bcf, width a080e2bc, both equal to the frozen run-2 pair; run alone 05:19-05:55)

`gate 14: ARM/Thumb flags: PASS (844 checks)`, `pcc2 default rows emitted 23`, `pcc2 default rows green 23`, `pcc2 width rows emitted 251`,
`pcc2 width rows green 251`, GATE_RC=0.  The previous run of this gate (04:26-05:00, `gate14_501_FAIL_rowformat.log`) had
counted ZERO default rows against a probe that said DONE -- the row-format defect described above -- and is kept.

## Pass 2 (nine seats on the real diff, `_scratchpad/panel_20260905_055656`): the 23-row detector was BELOW THE FLOOR, six ways

**Roster.**  codex (GPT-6 Astra, max), grok, kimi (the CLI stalled on the 54 KB pointer form -- 4.5 KB of paging
monologue, no answer, `kimi.stall_pointerform.txt` -- and was RE-FIRED ONCE with the 19 KB packet inline; it then
answered), glm and deepseek (thinking-burned on the full packet -- `ollama_*.burn.txt` -- and answered 2 KB
one-question mini packets), minimax, Opus 5 and Fable 5 (Agent seats; answers preserved as `opus5.txt`, `fable5.txt`).
agy DELIBERATELY NOT FIRED (measurement reserve).  Five seats said PASS or GO (kimi, minimax, glm, deepseek; Fable
GO-WITH-CHANGES, provisional); three said NOT CLOSE (codex, grok, Opus).  **Count the arguments: the three carried
measurements or predictions the five had not tested, and the Opus seat's two were MEASURED in its own scratch clone
against the frozen instrument pair (md5-verified equal to the live one).**  The fix itself every seat held right:
the fold bounds the shift (`uint64_t`, `%= 0x40`), `break` reaches the one `return 1`, the latch words are separate
fields both zeroed at DEVINIT, no cased arm is touched -- and, as Opus put it, the arm can only add output where the
old code called `exit(1)`, so no boot that works today can regress.

**The six escapes of the 23-row instrument** (every one a clause-(d)-shaped edit of the new arm; the first two
MEASURED at 23/23, the rest predicted from the row list and measured in table 3 below):

| seat | mutant | why 23 rows could not see it |
|---|---|---|
| Opus (MEASURED 23/23) | the read announce's value `-> 0` (one token) | `named rd srvd` counts the PHRASE `served 0x`, never the value; no row served a nonzero byte |
| Opus (MEASURED 23/23) | member 6 on the LATCHED path: `} else if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) pcctwo_reg[ra] = idata;` | both `rdawr` rows store ONCE to a fresh offset in a fresh process; the latch state was built by `sameoff`/`twooff`/`rw one` and observed on the COUNT channel only |
| grok | the latch SET conditioned on writes | the bit-set cell was reached only through a WRITE (`rw one`); two reads at one offset had no row -- the setter's direction is a third axis the (writeflag x latch) 2x2 hides |
| grok | `if (len != 1) exit(1);` -- the removed kill was width-agnostic, so this is a PARTIAL reinstatement | every default-path row was `st.b`/`ld.bu`; the pass-1 PRETABLE's own SIBLING PARITY sentence said "byte-and-wide" and no row drove wide |
| codex | `|=` `->` `=` at the latch update (forgets earlier bits when a new offset announces) | A,A and A,B and W,R never REVISIT an offset after another has announced; A,B,A wants 2 and the mutant prints 3 |
| Fable | `if (relative_addr == 0x06) exit(1);` -- a re-kill at an offset the rows never drove | writes drove 27 of the 43 offsets and reads 18 (codex's count); the pass-1 contract had DISCLOSED the sampling |

**The diagnosis, from the pass-1 PRETABLE's own GUARD-FIELD line:** *"they disagree only in the latch's own state,
which the sameoff/twooff/rw-one rows build"* -- true, and the state was observed on one channel (the announce count)
while the mutant that matters (Opus M_O2) changes the OTHER channel (the read-back value) in exactly that state.  The
guard/body check found the cell and the row was written against the wrong observable.  And codex found a product
defect the rows could not: the read announce RELOADED `pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]` after the response was copied, and
the SIGALRM tick rewrites the counter interiors -- so for the twelve in-word offsets the announced byte could differ
from the served one.  `"the array's default, NOT a computed value"` was therefore also wrong for those twelve.

**The repair (one diff, class closure -- the repair-chain amendment: consecutive cells of one class are closed
together, not one per pass).**  PRODUCT: both announces carry `(len %d)`; the read announce prints `data[0]` -- the
byte actually SERVED -- with a comment saying why.  DETECTOR, 36 rows (+13): `latch rd rd` and `latch rd wr` complete
the setter x tester product (W,W `sameoff`; W,R `rw one`; R,R; R,W); `latch aba` pins accumulation across offsets;
`sameoff val` reads back after the SECOND write (the latched path's disposition); `srvd val` + `srvd val text` serve
a byte the GUEST wrote (a word store through the cased T1CMP arm puts 0x34 at 0x05) and pin the announced value;
`wide wr` (+`msg`) and `wide rd` (+`srvd`) drive `st.w`/`ld.w` at uncased word-aligned offsets and pin the width
text; `inword wr`/`unnamed wr` now drive all 12 / all 13, and `inword rd`/`unnamed rd` read them -- **every one of
the 43 uncased offsets is written once and read once**; `wr declined` pins the word.  Row names stay under 14
characters (the gate's `%-16s` anchor).  RECORDS (the L4s the seats named): the kill-table header's `c76e053a ==
live` (run 1's md5 against run 2's table; the live probe was `a96b8bcf`); "three predictions exact" (four); the
M_EXIT diagnosis (its 16-row red SET was right, only the numeral and the derived score were wrong); "would have
shipped green and counted nothing" (the gate FAILED 25/844 and caught it); the `alias 0x6a` row saying "not proven
of the real part" beside a docstring citing Table 6-1; `unnamed wr` describing an overrun M_BACKED's end-span guard
prevents; "four arms up" (ten case blocks); "no guest reaches these offsets" (the witness IS a guest -- limited to
the inspected OpenBSD driver paths); "unnamed" made explicit as "the fork header has no label" (the manual names
0x39 as SPEED's second byte and 0x3b as PRTDATA's low byte -- codex).  Pass-2 mini-packet findings adopted: the
announce under-described a wide read (deepseek, grok, Opus, codex all named it) -- fixed by the width label.
Rejected: minimax's "unsafe" label on the M_RDKILL prediction miss (a predicted-green row that went red is the
SAFE direction, by the round's own rule).  Recorded, not adopted: codex's per-instance latch point is `pcc2latchinst`
(filed); Opus noted there is NO mvme88k boot rig (`grep -l mvme regress/*.sh` is empty), so the two gate-14 probes are
this device's entire coverage -- a fact for the queue, not this round.

## Table 3 (`killtable3_20260905/driver.log`; predictions in `PRETABLE_501b.out`, mtime 06:25:42, written before either run; frozen repaired instrument md5 `10737375` == live; the pass-2 instrument `old23/` md5 `a96b8bcf`; scratch clone of build/, BASE rebuilt in the clone, twelve mutants each compile-checked before the table and proven by a diff count)

**Every one of the twelve predictions was EXACT -- score and red set -- and every one of the six pass-2 escapes is
MEASURED on the same binary by both instruments: 23/23 on the instrument that shipped at pass 2, red on the repaired
one, on exactly the row built for it.**

Denominator of the zero: twelve mutant shapes, named above; the cells they close are the setter x tester direction
product (four), accumulation across offsets, the disposition on the latched path, the served VALUE, wide (len 4)
default accesses in both directions, and every one of the 43 uncased offsets driven once in each direction.  NOT
closed, disclosed in the contract: len-2-only re-kills; an end-spanning wide read; per-instance latch scope.

**Two runs, one prediction file.**  Run 1 (06:26-06:35) measured BASE (36/36, 23/23) and M_WRLATCH (34/36, 23/23) --
identical to run 2 -- and then DIED at its second `mut` line: an apostrophe inside a single-quoted expect string
(`len != 1'`) ended the quote and bash aborted the script with a parse error, while the wrapper reported rc=0 (its
trailing echo).  `mut_syntax2.py` had checked all twelve mutants' C and could not see a shell-quoting defect in the
driver; `bash -n` had been run on the gate script and not on the driver.  The partial run is kept as
`run1_partial_quoting/`; the generator now refuses a quote in an expect string and the launcher runs `bash -n` on
the frozen driver before exec.  Run 2 (06:37-07:26) is the table above.  The clone's source was `cmp`-clean against
SEC and its sentinel cleared after the restore rebuild.

## Gate 14 on the repaired instrument (`killtable3_20260905/gate14_501b.log`; the live probes -- default md5 `10737375` == the table-3 frozen instrument, width `a080e2bc`; run alone 07:39-08:16 on a build/ that had passed the two-part clearance minutes earlier)
```

## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)

**Do not run `run.sh`, any `gate_*.sh`, or the battery.** Every measurement you need is
inlined above. A gate takes minutes -- longer than your response budget -- and a second
concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone
else s. Building and running a single detector or mutant in your own temp directory is
fine and encouraged; driving the harness is not.

If you want a measurement that is not inlined, NAME IT and say why. Naming it is worth
far more to this panel than a timeout.

  20: 1. **The fix** (unchanged in substance since pass 1): the `default:` arm no longer calls `exit(1)`; a READ keeps the
  21:    byte the memcpy served; a WRITE is DECLINED (member 3, not member 6 -- the cased siblings contradict a blanket
  22:    store); one latched `fatal()` per offset per instance (`uint64_t warned_unimpl`, bit `relative_addr`, which is
  23:    folded `%= PCC2_SIZE` (0x40) as the handler's first statement so the shift is 0..63 on a 64-bit type).  43 of the
  24:    0x40 offsets reach the arm: 18 named-unimplemented, 12 counter interiors, 13 the fork header leaves unnamed (the
  25:    MANUAL names some of those: 0x39 is SPEED's second byte, 0x3b PRTDATA's low byte).
  26: 2. **The repair to the product** is confined to the arm: both announces gain `(len %d)`; the read announce prints
  27:    `data[0]` -- the byte actually SERVED -- instead of reloading `d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]`, because the SIGALRM
  28:    tick rewrites the counter interiors between the memcpy and the announce (codex), and a wide read is labelled.
  29:    `data` was filled by the memcpy above the switch (or zero-filled on the #119 end-span path, where `data[0]` is
  30:    then truthfully 0x00).  Three comment sentences corrected ("no guest reaches" -> the inspected OpenBSD driver
  31:    paths; "the array's default, not a computed value" -> the array's CURRENT byte, the tick's for an interior;
  32:    "four arms up" -> above).

  41:    repaired one (red).  BASE is measured full on both (the old anchors survive the `(len N)` text).

 241: ## THE ARM AS IT NOW STANDS (from `default:` to the handler's `return 1`, VERBATIM)
 242: 
 243: ```c
 244: 	default:
 245: 		/*  #501 (pcc2defaultkill): this arm ended the host with exit(1) on ANY
 246: 		    access to an offset with no case -- 43 of the 0x40 byte offsets:
 247: 		    eighteen documented registers the model does not implement (IEICR,
 248: 		    IEERR, IEBERR, GPIO, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, the *IACKs),
 249: 		    the bytes inside the four counter/compare words, and the unnamed
 250: 		    ones -- READS included, though the memcpy above the switch had
 251: 		    already served them.  A guest ld.bu or st.b at any of them was a host
 252: 		    kill (rung-3 witness).  Now: a READ keeps the served byte (the array's
 253: 		    current byte, said so -- for a counter interior that is the tick's
 254: 		    value, not a default); a WRITE is DECLINED.  Not a
 255: 		    backed store: the cased siblings contradict one (SCCERR clears on
 256: 		    write, the ICRs mask and reassert, PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are
 257: 		    read-only), no inspected OpenBSD driver path reaches them (if_ie never
 258: 		    attaches: unreadable sits at its 82596 address), and a store from the
 259: 		    one arm 43 offsets reach would add a bounded-write surface.  One
 260: 		    latched fatal() per offset per instance, the house `(once)' shape --
 261: 		    unlike PSCALEADJ's UNLATCHED print above (pcc2pscaleflood).  The
 262: 		    IACK offsets 0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest: modelling them (on
 263: 		    the SCCRXIACK shape) would open two new entries into the C040 path,
 264: 		    so pcc2c040kill goes first.  The %= fold above IS the real part's:
 265: 		    Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists $FFF42040-$FFF42FFF as repeated
 266: 		    PCCchip2 registers.  */
 267: 		if (!(d->warned_unimpl & (1ULL << relative_addr))) {
 268: 			d->warned_unimpl |= 1ULL << relative_addr;
 269: 			if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
 270: 				fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented write to offset "
 271: 				    "0x%x (len %d): 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\n",
 272: 				    (int) relative_addr, (int) len, (int) idata);
 273: 			else
 274: 				/*  data[0] is the byte actually SERVED; a reload of the
 275: 				    array here could differ (the tick rewrites the
 276: 				    counter interiors), and a wide read is labelled.  */
 277: 				fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented read from offset "
 278: 				    "0x%x (len %d) -- served 0x%02x.  (once) ]\n",
 279: 				    (int) relative_addr, (int) len, data[0]);
 280: 		}
 281: 		break;
 282: 	}
 283: 
 284: 	return 1;
 285: ```

 222: -				    (int) relative_addr, (int) idata);
 223: +				    "0x%x (len %d): 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\n",
 224: +				    (int) relative_addr, (int) len, (int) idata);
 225:  			else
 226: +				/*  data[0] is the byte actually SERVED; a reload of the
 227: +				    array here could differ (the tick rewrites the
 228: +				    counter interiors), and a wide read is labelled.  */
 229:  				fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented read from offset "

 231: -				    (int) relative_addr,
 232: -				    d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]);
 233: +				    "0x%x (len %d) -- served 0x%02x.  (once) ]\n",
 234: +				    (int) relative_addr, (int) len, data[0]);
 235:  		}
 236:  		break;
 237:  	}
 238: 
 239: ```
 240: 
 241: ## THE ARM AS IT NOW STANDS (from `default:` to the handler's `return 1`, VERBATIM)
 242: 
 243: ```c
 244: 	default:
 245: 		/*  #501 (pcc2defaultkill): this arm ended the host with exit(1) on ANY
 246: 		    access to an offset with no case -- 43 of the 0x40 byte offsets:
 247: 		    eighteen documented registers the model does not implement (IEICR,
 248: 		    IEERR, IEBERR, GPIO, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, the *IACKs),
 249: 		    the bytes inside the four counter/compare words, and the unnamed
 250: 		    ones -- READS included, though the memcpy above the switch had
 251: 		    already served them.  A guest ld.bu or st.b at any of them was a host
 252: 		    kill (rung-3 witness).  Now: a READ keeps the served byte (the array's
 253: 		    current byte, said so -- for a counter interior that is the tick's
 254: 		    value, not a default); a WRITE is DECLINED.  Not a
 255: 		    backed store: the cased siblings contradict one (SCCERR clears on
 256: 		    write, the ICRs mask and reassert, PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are
 257: 		    read-only), no inspected OpenBSD driver path reaches them (if_ie never
 258: 		    attaches: unreadable sits at its 82596 address), and a store from the
 259: 		    one arm 43 offsets reach would add a bounded-write surface.  One
 260: 		    latched fatal() per offset per instance, the house `(once)' shape --
 261: 		    unlike PSCALEADJ's UNLATCHED print above (pcc2pscaleflood).  The
 262: 		    IACK offsets 0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest: modelling them (on
 263: 		    the SCCRXIACK shape) would open two new entries into the C040 path,
 264: 		    so pcc2c040kill goes first.  The %= fold above IS the real part's:
 265: 		    Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists $FFF42040-$FFF42FFF as repeated
 266: 		    PCCchip2 registers.  */
 267: 		if (!(d->warned_unimpl & (1ULL << relative_addr))) {
 268: 			d->warned_unimpl |= 1ULL << relative_addr;
 269: 			if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
 270: 				fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented write to offset "
 271: 				    "0x%x (len %d): 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\n",
 272: 				    (int) relative_addr, (int) len, (int) idata);
 273: 			else
 274: 				/*  data[0] is the byte actually SERVED; a reload of the
 275: 				    array here could differ (the tick rewrites the
 276: 				    counter interiors), and a wide read is labelled.  */
 277: 				fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented read from offset "
 278: 				    "0x%x (len %d) -- served 0x%02x.  (once) ]\n",
 279: 				    (int) relative_addr, (int) len, data[0]);
 280: 		}
 281: 		break;
 282: 	}
 283: 
 284: 	return 1;
 285: ```

 459: (rung-3 witness, 7/13 on the committed build: regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/witness_20260905/).
 460: 
 461: THE FIX (pass-1 panel of 2026-09-05, nine seats, converged): READ side -- keep the byte the memcpy
 462: already served (the register array's CURRENT byte -- the tick's, for a counter interior: the announce says so);
 463: WRITE side -- DECLINE + a per-offset latched `fatal()' ("(once)", the house idiom), NOT a backed
 464: accept: the file's own cased siblings contradict a blanket store (SCCERR is write-to-clear, the ICRs
 465: mask and reassert, PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are read-only), the addition licence is unmet (no guest
 466: reaches these offsets on this model's inspected driver paths -- if_ie never attaches), and a store into pcctwo_reg[] from the
 467: one arm reached by 43 offsets adds a bounded-write surface the decline does not have.
 468: 
 469: CONTRACT -- what this detector is built to catch, by row (36 rows; the 13 added by the pass-2 repair are marked +):
 470:   (b) the verbatim `exit(1)' reinstated             -> every survival row (named/inword/unnamed wr AND rd, alias, wide)
 471:   (d) the announce unlatched                        -> `latch sameoff' (2 lines, not 1)
 472:   (d) one whole-arm latch bit instead of per-offset -> `latch twooff' (1 line, not 2), `named wr msg' (1, not 18)
 473:   (d) the latch SET conditioned on writes (grok A)  -> + `latch rd rd' (2, not 1), + `latch rd wr' (2, not 1): with
 474:       `latch sameoff' (W,W) and `latch rw one' (W,R) these are ALL FOUR setter x tester direction cells
 475:   (d) `|=' -> `=' at the latch update (codex)       -> + `latch aba' (3, not 2): bits already set survive a new offset
 476:   (d) a writeflag guard that re-kills READS         -> `named rd' (host exit)
 477:   (d) a `len != 1' re-kill -- the removed kill was width-agnostic, so this is a partial reinstatement (grok B)
 478:                                                     -> + `wide wr' / + `wide rd' (host exit)
 479:   (d) a re-kill at ONE unsampled offset (Fable)     -> `inword wr' / `unnamed wr' now drive all 12 / all 13, and
 480:                                                        + `inword rd' / + `unnamed rd' read them: every one of the 43
 481:                                                        uncased offsets is written once and read once
 482:   (d) the direction test inverted in the text       -> `dir wr text' / `dir rd text' / + `wr declined'
 483:   member 6 (BACKED ACCEPT) substituted for member 3 -> `ieicr rdawr' / `ieerr rdawr' read back the stored byte (0x5a)
 484:       instead of 0x0 -- the DISPOSITION-pinning rows; `sccerr rdawr' (a CASED, write-to-clear sibling) is the
 485:       control that reads 0x0 under either and makes the delta attributable
 486:   member 6 on the LATCHED path only (Opus M_O2: a store exempting the first write per offset -- MEASURED 23/23 on
 487:       the 23-row instrument)                        -> + `sameoff val' (0x5a, not 0x0)
 488:   the read announce printing a wrong or constant byte (Opus M_O1, MEASURED 23/23: `named rd srvd' counts the
 489:       phrase, not the value)                        -> + `srvd val text' (`served 0x34' absent); + `srvd val' pins
 490:       the served byte itself as one the GUEST wrote (through the cased T1CMP word arm), not a default
 491:   the announce omitting the access width            -> + `wide wr msg' / + `wide rd srvd' (`(len 4)')
 492:   the `relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE' fold changed      -> `alias msg' (the announce names offset 0x2a, not 0x6a)
 493:   the fix perturbing a cased arm                    -> `genctl rt', `t1cmp word rt'
 494: DISCLOSED, not caught: half-word (len 2) default accesses (the mutant that kills len 4 also kills len 2; one that
 495: kills ONLY len 2 is not rowed); an END-SPANNING wide read (0x3d + 4 > 0x40) takes the zero-fill path and is announced
 496: `served 0x00' truthfully (data[0] is what was served) but no row drives it; the announce names the FIRST served byte
 497: of a wide read, labelled with the width; the in-word bytes are the interior of IMPLEMENTED counters, declined here
 498: as a width/alignment matter (pcc2widecompose's business) -- and their served byte is the tick's, not a default,
 499: which is why the announce prints data[0] rather than reloading the array; the fold of 0x40..0xfff onto 0x00..0x3f
 500: IS the real part's: Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists $FFF42040-$FFF42FFF as 'PCCchip2 Registers  Repeated'
 501: (_scratchpad/vmesbcp2.txt:4629; two seats had rightly called it unestablished before the manual was on disk); the
 502: fork's PCC2_SIZE (0x40) and the guest header's (0x1000) name different things; the IACK offsets 0x23/0x25 are
 503: declined like the rest -- modelling them (on the SCCRXIACK shape) would open two new entries into the C040 path, so
 504: pcc2c040kill goes first; `named wr'/`named rd' touch all eighteen offsets in ONE session each (valid because the
 505: fix has no per-offset branch; the count rows are AGGREGATES, so a per-offset failure is located by reading the
 506: log, not by a row) -- if a later round branches per offset this compression is invalid; "unnamed" means the FORK
 507: HEADER has no label, not that the part is undocumented (0x39 is SPEED's second byte, 0x3b PRTDATA's low byte);
 508: per-instance latch scope is not provable by fresh sessions (each is a new process; `pcc2latchinst').
 509: The debugger's `put b' never reaches a DM_DEFAULT arm (measured): `put b ctl' is a control, not a witness.
 510: 
 511: Usage: python3 pcc2_default_probe.py <gxemul> <label>      Sentinel: PCC2D_PROBE_DONE
 512: The pty session helper is the width probe's own (extracted at run time), never hand-rolled: a first
 513: draft of the witness hand-rolled it and read every host kill as "survived".
 514: """
 515: import io, os, pty, re, select, sys, time
 516: BIN = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "build/gxemul"
 517: LABEL = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "local"
 518: DUMMY = "/tmp/pcc2_dummy.bin"
 519: if not os.path.exists(DUMMY):
 520:     open(DUMMY, "wb").write(b"\0" * 4096)
 521: HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
 522: _w = io.open(os.path.join(HERE, "pcc2_width_probe.py"), encoding="utf-8").read()
 523: exec(_w[_w.index("def session("):_w.index("\ndef printed")])     # session(cmds) -> (transcript, alive, how)
 524: 
 525: CODE, SCRATCH, PCC2, SENTINEL = 0x00010000, 0x00020000, 0xfff42000, 0xdeadbeef
 526: CHIPID, GENCTL, T1CMP, SCCERR = 0x00, 0x02, 0x04, 0x1c
 527: IEERR, IEICR, IEBERR, SCCTXIACK, PRTICR = 0x28, 0x2a, 0x2b, 0x25, 0x30
 528: NAMED18 = [0x14, 0x18, 0x19, 0x23, 0x25, 0x28, 0x2a, 0x2b, 0x2c, 0x30, 0x31, 0x32, 0x33, 0x34, 0x36, 0x37, 0x38, 0x3a]
 529: INWORD = [0x05, 0x06, 0x07, 0x09, 0x0a, 0x0b, 0x0d, 0x0e, 0x0f, 0x11, 0x12, 0x13]   # all 12 counter interiors
 530: UNNAMED = [0x20, 0x21, 0x22, 0x24, 0x26, 0x29, 0x2d, 0x2e, 0x35, 0x39, 0x3b, 0x3c, 0x3d]   # all 13 the fork header leaves unnamed
 531: assert len(NAMED18) + len(INWORD) + len(UNNAMED) == 43 and len(set(NAMED18 + INWORD + UNNAMED)) == 43
 532: MSG = "pcc2: unimplemented"
 533: def enc(op26, rd, rs1, imm): return (op26 << 26) | (rd << 21) | (rs1 << 16) | (imm & 0xffff)
 534: def LD_BU(rd, off): return enc(0x03, rd, 2, off)
 535: def LD_W(rd, off): return enc(0x05, rd, 2, off)
 536: def ST_B(rs, off): return enc(0x0b, rs, 2, off)
 537: def ST_W(rs, off): return enc(0x09, rs, 2, off)
 538: I_ST_SCR = enc(0x09, 5, 3, 0); I_LD_SCR = enc(0x05, 4, 3, 0); I_BR_SELF = 0xc0000000
 539: def program(insns, at=CODE):
 540:     words = list(insns) + [I_BR_SELF, 0]
 541:     return ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * k, w) for k, w in enumerate(words)] + ["pc=0x%x" % at, "step %d" % len(insns)]
 542: def setup(r5="0x5a5a5a5a"):
 543:     return ["r2=0x%x" % PCC2, "r3=0x%x" % SCRATCH, "r5=%s" % r5, "r4=0x%x" % SENTINEL]
 544: def printed(t, reg="r4"):
 545:     m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\r?\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
 546:     return "0x%x" % int(m.group(1), 16) if m else "absent"
 547: def fate(alive, how): return "survived" if alive else "host " + how
 548: def val(t, alive, how): return printed(t) if alive else fate(alive, how)
 549: rows, bad = [], []
 550: def row(name, got, want, why):
 551:     ok = str(got) == str(want)
 552:     rows.append(name); (None if ok else bad.append(name))
 553:     #  STATUS LAST: gate 14 counts a row with `^<name>  .*ok$' / ` ok$' / ` FAIL$' -- a first draft printed the
 554:     #  status before the note and the gate counted ZERO of 23 rows while the probe said DONE (caught by the gate, L1).
 555:     print("%-16s %-14s %-14s %-60s %s" % (name, got, want, why[:60], "ok" if ok else "FAIL"), flush=True)
 556: print("pcc2defaultkill DETECTOR -- binary=%s label=%s" % (BIN, LABEL))
 557: print("%-16s %-14s %-14s %-60s %s" % ("row", "got", "want", "note", ""))
 558: 
 559: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([I_ST_SCR, I_LD_SCR]) + ["print r4"])
 560: row("live", val(t, alive, how), "0x5a5a5a5a", "LIVENESS: scratch RAM st/ld round-trip")
 561: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([LD_BU(4, CHIPID)]) + ["print r4"])
 562: row("devsig chipid", val(t, alive, how), "0x20", "DEVICE-SIGNATURE: CHIPID reads PCC2_ID through a guest ld.bu (RAM would read 0)")
 563: # -- the eighteen named unimplemented registers, one session each direction
 564: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, o) for o in NAMED18]))
 565: row("named wr", fate(alive, how), "survived", "guest st.b at all 18 named unimplemented registers in ONE session: (b) reinstated exit(1) kills on the first")
 566: row("named wr msg", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 18, "one announce PER OFFSET: a whole-arm latch prints 1, an unlatched arm prints 18 too -- see `latch sameoff' (an aggregate count, not a locator)")
 567: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([LD_BU(4, o) for o in NAMED18]))
 568: row("named rd", fate(alive, how), "survived", "guest ld.bu at all 18 in one session: the READ side of the defect; a writeflag guard that re-kills reads dies here")
 569: row("named rd msg", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 18, "the read announce fires once per offset too")
 570: row("named rd srvd", t.count("served 0x") if alive else "n/a", 18, "the read announce says what happened -- SERVED, never 'ignored' (the VALUE is pinned by `srvd val text')")
 571: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, o) for o in INWORD]))
 572: row("inword wr", fate(alive, how), "survived", "ALL twelve bytes inside the four 32-bit counter/compare words: no case -> default: (a re-kill at any one dies)")
 573: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([LD_BU(4, o) for o in INWORD]))
 574: row("inword rd", fate(alive, how), "survived", "...and a guest ld.bu at each of the twelve (reads drove 18/43 offsets before this row -- codex, Fable)")
 575: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, o) for o in UNNAMED]))
 576: row("unnamed wr", fate(alive, how), "survived", "ALL thirteen offsets the fork header does not name (the manual names some: 0x39 is SPEED's 2nd byte, 0x3b PRTDATA's)")
 577: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([LD_BU(4, o) for o in UNNAMED]))
 578: row("unnamed rd", fate(alive, how), "survived", "...and a guest ld.bu at each of the thirteen: with named rd + inword rd every one of the 43 is read once")
 579: # -- the disposition-pinning rows: read-after-write in ONE session
 580: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), LD_BU(4, IEICR)]) + ["print r4"])
 581: row("ieicr rdawr", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "st.b 0x5a then ld.bu at IEICR in ONE session reads 0x0: the write was DECLINED (a backed accept reads 0x5a)")
 582: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEERR), LD_BU(4, IEERR)]) + ["print r4"])
 583: row("ieerr rdawr", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "the same at IEERR (0x28), the write-to-clear class whose cased sibling SCCERR clears on write")
 584: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, SCCERR), LD_BU(4, SCCERR)]) + ["print r4"])
 585: row("sccerr rdawr", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "CONTROL: the CASED write-to-clear sibling reads 0x0 under either disposition -- makes the two rows above attributable")
 586: # -- the latch: per offset, per instance
 587: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), ST_B(5, IEICR), LD_BU(4, IEICR)]) + ["print r4"])
 588: row("latch sameoff", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 1, "two writes to one offset announce ONCE: an unlatched arm prints 2 (the read-back is silent: bit set)")
 589: row("sameoff val", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "...and the SECOND write (latch already set) is declined too: a store exempting the first write reads 0x5a (Opus M_O2, MEASURED 23/23 escape)")
 590: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), ST_B(5, IEBERR)]))
 591: row("latch twooff", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 2, "two offsets announce TWICE: a single whole-arm latch bit prints 1")
 592: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), LD_BU(4, IEICR)]))
 593: row("latch rw one", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 1, "a write then a read at ONE offset announce once: the WRITE sets the bit the READ tests")
 594: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([LD_BU(4, IEICR), LD_BU(4, IEICR)]))
 595: row("latch rd rd", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 1, "two READS at one offset announce once: a latch SET only on writes prints 2 (grok A) -- the setter x tester cell rw one cannot see")
 596: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([LD_BU(4, IEICR), ST_B(5, IEICR)]))
 597: row("latch rd wr", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 1, "a READ then a write at one offset announce once: the read's bit silences the write (the fourth setter x tester cell)")
 598: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), ST_B(5, IEBERR), ST_B(5, IEICR)]))
 599: row("latch aba", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 2, "A,B,A announces TWICE: `|=' -> `=' forgets A when B announces and prints 3 (codex) -- earlier bits survive a new offset")
 600: # -- the fold and the text
 601: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, 0x6a)]))
 602: row("alias 0x6a", fate(alive, how), "survived", "0x6a folds onto 0x2a (`relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE'): the real part's fold too (VMESBCP2 Table 6-1, `Repeated')")
 603: row("alias msg", t.count("offset 0x2a") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...and the announce names the FOLDED offset 0x2a: a changed fold names another")
 604: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, PRTICR)]))
 605: row("dir wr text", t.count("unimplemented write to") if alive else "n/a", 1, "the announce says WRITE for a store (an inverted writeflag test in the text says read)")
 606: row("wr declined", t.count("declined") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...and the word is DECLINED (no other row counts it: `ignored' would pass every count row)")
 607: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([LD_BU(4, PRTICR)]))
 608: row("dir rd text", t.count("unimplemented read from") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...and READ for a load")
 609: # -- the served VALUE: a guest word store to the CASED T1CMP puts 0x34 in its interior byte 0x05, then an uncased ld.bu there
 610: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x12345678") + program([ST_W(5, T1CMP), LD_BU(4, 0x05)]) + ["print r4"])
 611: row("srvd val", val(t, alive, how), "0x34", "the served byte is the array's CURRENT byte, here one the guest wrote through the cased word arm -- not a default")
 612: row("srvd val text", t.count("served 0x34") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...and the announce prints THAT byte (data[0], what was served): `served 0x%02x' of 0 passes `named rd srvd' (Opus M_O1, MEASURED)")
 613: # -- wide accesses at default offsets: the old kill was width-agnostic, so a `len != 1' re-kill is a partial reinstatement (grok B)
 614: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a5a5a5a") + program([ST_W(5, IEERR), LD_W(4, IEERR)]) + ["print r4"])
 615: row("wide wr", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "a guest st.w at IEERR (0x28..0x2b, all uncased) survives and is declined: reads back 0x0 (a memcpy-backed store reads 0x5a5a5a5a)")
 616: row("wide wr msg", t.count("offset 0x28 (len 4)") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...announced once, with the width; the ld.w at the same offset is silenced by the per-offset bit")
 617: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([LD_W(4, PRTICR)]) + ["print r4"])
 618: row("wide rd", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "a guest ld.w at PRTICR (0x30..0x33, all uncased) survives and is served the four zero bytes")
 619: row("wide rd srvd", t.count("(len 4) -- served 0x00") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...and the announce names the width and the first served byte")
 620: # -- the cased arms are untouched
 621: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x82") + program([ST_B(5, GENCTL), LD_BU(4, GENCTL)]) + ["print r4"])
 622: row("genctl rt", val(t, alive, how), "0x82", "an IMPLEMENTED byte register still round-trips (no C040 bit in 0x82)")
 623: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x12345678") + program([ST_W(5, T1CMP), LD_W(4, T1CMP)]) + ["print r4"])
 624: row("t1cmp word rt", val(t, alive, how), "0x12345678", "the cased 32-bit counter arm still round-trips a word: the fix swallowed no cased arm")
 625: t, alive, how = session(["put b 0xfff4202a, 0x00"])
 626: row("put b ctl", fate(alive, how), "survived", "CONTROL: the debugger's put b never reaches a DM_DEFAULT arm (measured before the fix too)")
 627: row("put b ctl msg", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 0, "...so it prints nothing -- not a witness, a control")
 628: print("PCC2D_RESULT=%d/%d" % (len(rows) - len(bad), len(rows)))
 629: if bad:
 630:     print("PCC2D_RED=%s" % ",".join(bad))
 631: print("PCC2D_PROBE_DONE")
 632: 
 633: ```
 634: 
 635: ## THE GATE SECTION (`git diff -- regress/gate_arm.sh`, VERBATIM; the whole #501 section is new against HEAD)

 638: diff --git a/regress/gate_arm.sh b/regress/gate_arm.sh
 639: index 65930c2..c542455 100644
 640: --- a/regress/gate_arm.sh
 641: +++ b/regress/gate_arm.sh
 642: @@ -1303,6 +1303,31 @@ else
 643:  fi
 644:  
 645:  
 646: +#  ------------------------------------------------------------ pcc2defaultkill (#501)
 647: +#  The default: arm of DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) ended the host on any access to an uncased
 648: +#  offset, reads included.  Now: reads keep the served byte, writes are declined, one
 649: +#  latched announce per offset per instance.  The witness (regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/

 654: +    check "pcc2 default probe completed" "no" "yes"

 667: +    check "pcc2 default rows red"     "$(count "$PCC2DLOG" " FAIL$")" "0"
 668: +fi
 669: +

 676: 
 677: ## THE CHANGELOG BLOCK (as it stands)
 678: 
 679: ```
 680: # Round #501 pcc2defaultkill -- the `default:` arm of DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) ended the host on any access to an uncased offset, reads included; now reads keep the served byte and writes are declined, one latched announce per offset
 681: 
 682: **2026-09-05.  HIGH, ranked first by the 2026-09-04 fix-review adjudication.  Product change: `src/devices/dev_pcc2.c`
 683: -- the `exit(1)` replaced by a per-offset latched `fatal()` (`uint64_t warned_unimpl`, one bit per byte offset) that
 684: says DECLINED for a write and SERVED 0x%02x for a read, then `break` to the handler's single `return 1`.  Detector:
 685: `regress/pcc2_default_probe.py`, 23 rows, run by gate 14.  Witness: rung 3, measured before the edit.**
 686: 
 687: ## The defect and its witness
 688: 
 689: Forty-three of the 0x40 byte offsets have no `case` (derived from `mvme_pcctworeg.h` against the 21 labels; two seats
 690: re-derived it): eighteen NAMED registers the model does not implement (PSCALECNT, GPIO_ICR/PCR, SCCMOIACK, SCCTXIACK,
 691: IEERR, IEICR, IEBERR, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, PRTDATA), the twelve bytes INSIDE the four 32-bit
 692: counter/compare words, and thirteen unnamed offsets.  Any access to one of them -- a READ included, although the
 693: memcpy above the switch had already served it -- reached `default:` and `exit(1)`.
 694: 
 695: **The witness (`regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/witness_20260905/`), measured on the committed build BEFORE the
 696: edit: 7/13.**  A guest `st.b` to IEICR (0x2a) ends the host with exit status 1, the arm's own `pcc2: unimplemented`
 697: line printed first (the arm was reached); a guest `ld.bu` there likewise -- the READ side kills too; IEBERR and the

 706: ## The design (pass 1: nine seats, `_scratchpad/panel_20260905_021751`, converged)
 707: 
 708: READ: keep the byte the memcpy served and SAY it is served -- it is the register array's default, not a computed
 709: value, so this is not the option set's member 4 and a bounded announce about it is not a wrong record.  WRITE:
 710: member 3, DECLINE + latched announce -- NOT member 6 (backed accept), on three warrants the seats measured or read:
 711: the file's own cased siblings contradict a blanket store (SCCERR is write-to-clear -- the board manual's SCLR bits
 712: read as zero; the ICRs mask and reassert; PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are read-only); the addition licence is unmet
 713: (no guest reaches these offsets on this model); a store from the one arm 43 offsets reach adds a bounded-write
 714: surface into `pcctwo_reg[]` at 0x3d.  The Opus seat BUILT both candidates in a scratch clone and measured the
 715: 13-row witness at 13/13 under BOTH -- disposition-blind -- so the detector carries read-after-write rows in ONE
 716: session (`ieicr rdawr`, `ieerr rdawr`: store 0x5a then load, want 0x0 -- a backed accept reads 0x5a) with the CASED
 717: write-to-clear sibling `sccerr rdawr` as the control that reads 0x0 under either.
 718: 
 719: **Two premises of the pass-1 brief were wrong and are corrected here.**  The in-tree guest driver (`if_ie.c`) writes
 720: three of these offsets, but NEVER RUNS on this model: `machine_mvme88k.c:106` installs `unreadable` at the 82596
 721: address, `if_ie.c:407-409` fails its match on `badpaddr()` there, and `ie_setupram()` returns early at `:561-564`
 722: before the PCC2 store at `:609` (Fable, Opus, grok, codex independently; verified).  `ie0` IS configured in
 723: `conf/M187` and `conf/NFS` -- the brief's conf grep looked in the wrong files.  So the HIGH ranking rests on the
 724: witness and severity, not on the driver.  And the alias the model implements (`relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE`) is the
 725: REAL part's: Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists `$FFF42040-$FFF42FFF  PCCchip2 Registers  Repeated` -- the codex seat
 726: cited it, the manual was fetched into `_scratchpad/` and the line read (`vmesbcp2.txt:4629`).  Two seats had rightly
 727: called the fold unestablished before the manual was on disk.
 728: 
 729: Recorded alternatives: the codex seat's ONE latch for the whole site, writes only (not adopted: a driver scanning
 730: registers should see each distinct one announced; both forms are bounded).  Corrections to the option set from this
 731: round: `return 0` denotes a FAILED TRANSACTION, not inherently unmapped hardware (an IACK cycle with no interrupt
 732: pending times out on the real part).  Disclosed: the in-word bytes are the interior of IMPLEMENTED counters, declined
 733: here as a width/alignment matter (`pcc2widecompose`), not claimed as unimplemented registers; the IACK offsets
 734: 0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest -- modelling them on the SCCRXIACK shape would open two new entries into the
 735: C040 path, so `pcc2c040kill` goes first (filed as a cross-round obligation); per-instance latch scope is not
 736: provable by two fresh sessions (each is a new process) -- a two-instance fixture is filed.

 811: **The diagnosis, from the pass-1 PRETABLE's own GUARD-FIELD line:** *"they disagree only in the latch's own state,
 812: which the sameoff/twooff/rw-one rows build"* -- true, and the state was observed on one channel (the announce count)
 813: while the mutant that matters (Opus M_O2) changes the OTHER channel (the read-back value) in exactly that state.  The
 814: guard/body check found the cell and the row was written against the wrong observable.  And codex found a product
 815: defect the rows could not: the read announce RELOADED `pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]` after the response was copied, and
 816: the SIGALRM tick rewrites the counter interiors -- so for the twelve in-word offsets the announced byte could differ
 817: from the served one.  `"the array's default, NOT a computed value"` was therefore also wrong for those twelve.

 820: together, not one per pass).**  PRODUCT: both announces carry `(len %d)`; the read announce prints `data[0]` -- the
 821: byte actually SERVED -- with a comment saying why.  DETECTOR, 36 rows (+13): `latch rd rd` and `latch rd wr` complete
 822: the setter x tester product (W,W `sameoff`; W,R `rw one`; R,R; R,W); `latch aba` pins accumulation across offsets;
 823: `sameoff val` reads back after the SECOND write (the latched path's disposition); `srvd val` + `srvd val text` serve
 824: a byte the GUEST wrote (a word store through the cased T1CMP arm puts 0x34 at 0x05) and pin the announced value;

 827: the 43 uncased offsets is written once and read once**; `wr declined` pins the word.  Row names stay under 14

 918: ## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)
 919: 
 920: **Do not run `run.sh`, any `gate_*.sh`, or the battery.** Every measurement you need is
 921: inlined above. A gate takes minutes -- longer than your response budget -- and a second
 922: concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
 923: log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone
 924: else s. Building and running a single detector or mutant in your own temp directory is
 925: fine and encouraged; driving the harness is not.
 926: 
 927: If you want a measurement that is not inlined, NAME IT and say why. Naming it is worth
 928: far more to this panel than a timeout.

  65: // #define	debug fatal
  66: 
  67: /*  pcc2widthkills: TWELVE width-guard blocks in DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) -- TEN of them
  68:     `fatal(); exit(1)' kills that ended the host on a guest-chosen access width, and
  69:     two (IPL, MASK) #119 warn-once latches that declined it process-globally -- an ordinary aligned halfword load of a documented
  70:     register.  Same class as #337 (dev_wdc.c) and #497 (dev_sgi_re.c).
  71: 
  72:     THE TREE ALREADY KNEW THE WIDTH IS LEGAL: mvme_pcctworeg.h says the PCCchip2
  73:     registers "can be accessed as bytes (8 bits), two-bytes (16 bits), or four-bytes
  74:     (32 bits)".  So this is not a fault to model; it is an access the model declines
  75:     to IMPLEMENT on one side and already implements on the other:
  76: 
  77:       READS   are served BEFORE the switch by memcpy(data, pcctwo_reg + addr, len),
  78:               raw bytes in address order -- width-correct by construction.  The old
  79:               guard discarded a correct answer.  Now: ACCEPT (fall through).
  80:       WRITES  ten of the twelve arms use `idata', the composed value (SCCERR stores a
  81:               constant and IPL ignores it; both decline too -- a wide write's other
  82:               lanes have no home), and `idata' is "the addressed
  83:               byte" ONLY at len == 1: memory_readmax64 composes big-endian, so
  84:               (uint8_t) idata is the HIGHEST-address lane.  Deleting the guard would
  85:               store the neighbour's byte -- st.h 0xAABB to GENCTL would set GENCTL to
  86:               0xBB, VECBASE's lane.  And eight arms mask, read-modify-write or clear
  87:               on write, and seven of the twelve blocks call reassert_interrupts()
  88:               (five of those eight; a seat measured the earlier "six" wrong).  Now: DECLINE +
  89:               latched announce.  Per-lane composition through each arm (COMPOSE) is
  90:               the faithful upgrade and is FILED as pcc2widecompose; not taken here
  91:               because the hardware's lane semantics are not on disk and no driver
  92:               MEASUREMENT exists either way: the only consumer is mvme187, no mvme187
  93:               boot runs in this harness, and the one OS source in the tree (OpenBSD
  94:               2.2) declares these registers `volatile u_char' and byte-accesses them
  95:               -- a reading, not a measurement.
  96: 
  97:     ONE BIT PER BLOCK, twelve, per instance: a driver bringing up the chip touches
  98:     several of these in one pass, and one latch would announce the first and silence
  99:     the rest (#497's own reasoning).  Auto-incremented rather than `1 << N' literals,
 100:     which this fork records as collision-prone; PCC2_W_NBITS names the count, and the
 101:     typedef after the enum makes the build fail if it ever exceeds the 32-bit field.
 102: 
 103:     REACHABILITY, verified: every non-byte m88k access is alignment-checked BEFORE
 104:     memory_rw, and `relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE' keeps parity, so only the SIX
 105:     EVEN-offset blocks (GENCTL, TxCTL, TxICR, SCCERR, SCCTX, IPL) are GUEST-reachable
 106:     (this said "five" until pass 2; IPL at 0x3e is even, and a seat counted); the
 107:     eight odd OFFSETS -- the six odd-only blocks VECBASE, PSCALEADJ, SCCICR, SCCRX,
 108:     SCSIICR, MASK, plus the T1CTL/T1ICR labels of the shared TxCTL/TxICR blocks --
 109:     are OPERATOR-reachable -- the debugger's `put h' warns on a
 110:     misaligned address and stores anyway, MEASURED (artifacts/pcc2widthkills/
 111:     operator_reach_20260903.out: `put h 0xfff42003' ended the host).  All twelve are
 112:     converted; the detector reaches the odd ones through that path.
 113: 
 114:     NOT FIXED HERE, cited so "twelve kills removed" is not read as "file clean":
 115:     pcc2c040kill (a LEGAL byte write of 0x04 to GENCTL still ends the host in
 116:     reassert_interrupts) and pcc2defaultkill (an unlisted offset still ends it).  */
 117: enum {
 118: 	PCC2_W_GENCTL_LEN,
 119: 	PCC2_W_VECBASE_LEN,
 120: 	PCC2_W_PSCALEADJ_LEN,
 121: 	PCC2_W_TXCTL_LEN,	/*  one block serves T1CTL and T2CTL  */
 122: 	PCC2_W_TXICR_LEN,	/*  one block serves T1ICR and T2ICR  */
 123: 	PCC2_W_SCCERR_LEN,
 124: 	PCC2_W_SCCICR_LEN,
 125: 	PCC2_W_SCCTX_LEN,
 126: 	PCC2_W_SCCRX_LEN,
 127: 	PCC2_W_SCSIICR_LEN,
 128: 	PCC2_W_IPL_LEN,		/*  was a #119 function-static: process-global  */
 129: 	PCC2_W_MASK_LEN,	/*  was a #119 function-static: process-global  */
 130: 	PCC2_W_NBITS
 131: };
